```tsx
  const warm = () => void prefetchSessionOpen(queryClient, projectId, sessionId);
  return <a href={`/sessions/${sessionId}`} onPointerEnter={warm} onFocus={warm} />;
}
```

Call it on hover, focus or touch of a session link, or when a route names a session. It never calls `/start`, so it never wakes a sandbox. It never rejects. A second call for the same session within 30 seconds sends nothing.

#### Render from the device on a reload

Two stores keep what a person already saw, so a reload or a cold start paints it in the first frame. The server's answer then refreshes it in place. Both stores are per user and bounded. They come from `@kortix/sdk`.

| Store | Keeps | Default bounds |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `createPersistedQueryCache({ storage, userId, shouldPersist })` | React Query results that `shouldPersist` accepts. `isPersistableQueryKey` accepts accounts, projects, project detail, session lists and session rows. A restored entry refetches when it is first read. | 1,000,000 characters, 7 days |
| `createSavedCopyStore({ storage, userId })`, registered with `setSavedCopyStore` | The saved transcript copies the server sent. `useSession` paints a kept copy before the server answers. A copy over the per-copy limit keeps its newest messages. | 12 sessions, 1,500,000 characters, 300,000 characters per copy, 14 days |

```ts
import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  browserKeyValueStorage,
  createPersistedQueryCache,
  createSavedCopyStore,
  isPersistableQueryKey,
  setSavedCopyStore,
} from '@kortix/sdk';

export function adoptDeviceCaches(queryClient: QueryClient, userId: string) {
  const storage = browserKeyValueStorage(); // null where localStorage is blocked or absent
  if (!storage) return null;
  const cache = createPersistedQueryCache({ storage, userId, shouldPersist: isPersistableQueryKey });
  cache.restore(queryClient); // synchronous with localStorage
  const stop = cache.persist(queryClient);
  window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => void cache.flush());
  const copies = createSavedCopyStore({ storage, userId });
  setSavedCopyStore(copies);
  return { cache, copies, stop };
}
```

- `persist` writes at most once per second. Call `flush()` on `pagehide`, so a reload keeps the last change.
- On React Native, pass AsyncStorage as `storage`. `restore` then returns a promise.
- The saved-copy store keeps only copies the server sent at a turn end, never the live transcript. A stopped turn cannot come back as a running one.
- A full, corrupt or missing store never throws. The app then opens from the server, as without the store.
- `isPersistableQueryKey` never keeps a permission probe (`useCan`). When a probe's answer picks a query key, read the entry the restored cache holds until the probe answers. The web Sessions page does this for its manager-only `scope: 'project'` list.

### Sign-out and identity changes

The SDK keeps per-person session state in memory: transcripts, open questions and permissions, turn receipts and model picks. On every identity change, clear it and your caches in one handler. An identity change is a sign-out, a different person signing in, and a sign-in in another tab that changes the person without a page load.

```ts
import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { setSavedCopyStore } from '@kortix/sdk';
import { resetIdentityState } from '@kortix/sdk/react';

export async function onIdentityChange(queryClient: QueryClient, device: ReturnType<typeof adoptDeviceCaches>) {
  setSavedCopyStore(null);
  device?.stop();
  queryClient.clear();
  resetIdentityState();
  await device?.cache.clear();
  await device?.copies.clear();
}
```

`resetIdentityState()` never throws. It also removes the model picks from `localStorage`. It does not clear React Query, your own stores or the token source you passed to `createKortix`. Reset those in the same handler. Neither device store ever restores data for another person, but a signed-out device must not keep it.

## Kortix UI

`@kortix/ui` is the React component library that the Kortix web app renders, and the whole Kortix session experience as modules: the session workspace (thread plus side panel), the composer, files and the file viewer, the terminal, the project home and the session list. The same package serves Kortix Apps, white-label hosts and your own app, so a button, a chat thread or a session screen looks and behaves the same everywhere. Components are Tailwind CSS v4 class strings that read CSS variables. You re-theme them with a typed theme (`@kortix/ui/theme`) or by overriding the variables, never by copying a component.

### Install

```bash
npm i @kortix/ui @kortix/sdk
```

`@kortix/ui` and `@kortix/sdk` carry the Kortix release version. Install the same version of each. The module entries (`@kortix/ui/session`, `/workspace`, `/theme` and the others in [Modules](#modules)) ship from 0.14.4. 0.14.3 has the root, the component subpaths and the stylesheets. Inside the [kortix-ai/suna](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna) repository, add `"@kortix/ui": "workspace:*"` to your package's dependencies instead.

| Import | Holds |
| --- | --- |
| `@kortix/ui` | The light core: `cn`, `UiProvider`, `useUiStrings`, `useUiLink`, `useUiBrand`, `defaultUiStrings`, `useIsDark`, and the primitives `Button`, `Badge`, `Card`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `Label`, `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `Select`, `Modal`, `Hint`, `Popover`, `DropdownMenu`, `Tabs`, `Separator`, `Skeleton`, `Bubble`, `Message`, `Marker` with their parts, plus `FilterBar` / `FilterBarItem` (the pill row of `tabs`). It also exports `Dialog` and `Tooltip`, the primitives `Modal` and `Hint` build on; they are marked `@deprecated` for feature code. |
| `@kortix/ui/<component>` | One component file, for example `@kortix/ui/modal` or `@kortix/ui/dot-matrix/session-dot-matrix`. Heavy components (charts, maps, data grids, diagrams, the emoji picker) load only from their own subpath. |
| `@kortix/ui/session` | The session thread: `SessionThread`, its building blocks, `ModelSelector` and `AgentSelector`. It needs `@kortix/sdk` and its React peers. |
| `@kortix/ui/<module>` | One curated entry per module: `workspace`, `composer`, `session-panel`, `files`, `file-viewer`, `markdown`, `genui`, `diff`, `changes`, `terminal`, `browser`, `apps`, `access`, `customize`, `icons`, `i18n`. See [Modules](#modules). |
| `@kortix/ui/theme` | The typed theme: `KortixTheme`, `themeToCss`, `themeToCssVars`, `ThemeScope`. See [Theming](#theming). |
| `@kortix/ui` (helpers) | The helpers the components use come from the root too: `cn`, `useIsMobile`, `useDebounce`, `lazyClient`, `setUiTelemetry`, `setUiHostEnv`, `setUiRoutes` (the URL of every page the components link to; see [Routes](#routes)). |
| `@kortix/ui/tailwind.css`, `/tokens.css`, `/theme.css`, `/styles.css`, `/fonts.css` | The stylesheets. Load them as the next section says. |

`react` and `react-dom` 19 are peer dependencies (the PowerPoint preview needs React 19.2.7 or later). Install the rest by what you use:

| You use | Also install |
| --- | --- |
| `@kortix/ui/tailwind.css` (a Tailwind v4 host) | `tw-animate-css`, `tailwind-scrollbar`, `tailwind-scrollbar-hide` (optional peers: a host without Tailwind never installs Tailwind) |
| `@kortix/ui/session`, or any module entry that renders project or session data | `@kortix/sdk` and `@tanstack/react-query` 5, plus one `QueryClientProvider` ([React setup of the SDK](/docs/#set-up)). Without `@tanstack/react-query`, the bundler fails on an import inside `@kortix/sdk/react`. |
| The session thread (it renders [generative UI](/docs/#generative-ui) blocks) | `@openuidev/lang-core@0.3.1` and `zod`, the optional `@kortix/sdk` peers of `@kortix/sdk/genui`. Without them, `vite build` fails with `Missing export` in `@kortix/sdk/dist/genui`. |

The full install for a session host:

```bash
npm i @kortix/ui @kortix/sdk @tanstack/react-query @openuidev/lang-core@0.3.1 zod @phosphor-icons/react@2.1.10 react@19 react-dom@19
```

<Callout type="info">
Name `react@19 react-dom@19` in the install. `@phosphor-icons/react@2.1.10` is the icon set the samples below import: pin the version `@kortix/ui` uses, so the host and the components share one copy and `IconProvider` reaches your icons too. Under pnpm a transitive copy does not resolve from your code. An unversioned `react` lets npm pick React 18 for `cmdk@0.2.1`'s React 18 peer, and the install then does not finish. `Command` works on React 19. A host with `strict-peer-dependencies` must allow `cmdk`'s React peer.
</Callout>

<Callout type="info">
Server rendering: the entries that render sessions or files (`session`, `composer`, `session-panel`, `workspace`, `files`, `file-viewer`, `markdown`, `genui`, `customize`) import CSS (KaTeX and others) from JS, so plain Node cannot evaluate them (`ERR_UNKNOWN_FILE_EXTENSION ".css"`). A Vite SSR host adds `ssr: { noExternal: ['@kortix/ui'] }`. A Next.js host lists `@kortix/ui` in `transpilePackages`. Or load those blocks on the client only (`lazyClient`). The root, `theme`, `i18n`, `icons`, `diff`, `changes`, `terminal`, `browser`, `apps` and `access` entries evaluate in Node.
</Callout>

### Add the styles

Load the styles once, in your app's main stylesheet or entry file. Pick the row for your host.

<Tabs>
<Tab title="Tailwind CSS v4 host">
Import `@kortix/ui/tailwind.css` after Tailwind. It brings the tokens (`tokens.css`), the component layer (`theme.css`: the `dark` variant, the `@theme` keys the component classes resolve against, the utilities and the keyframes), the animation utilities and the scrollbar plugins. `theme.css` also adds an `@source` rule, so Tailwind scans the package for class names.

```css title="app.css"
@import 'tailwindcss';
@import '@kortix/ui/tailwind.css';
```

Your own classes resolve against the same tokens: `bg-background`, `text-muted-foreground`, `border-border`.

<Callout type="warning">
`tokens.css` sets `--spacing: 0.23rem`, 8% tighter than Tailwind's `0.25rem`. Your own spacing utilities follow it: `p-4` is 14.72px, not 16px. To keep stock Tailwind spacing for your own classes, set `--spacing: 0.25rem` in `:root` after the import. The components then render 8% larger.
</Callout>

To bring your own token values instead of `tokens.css`, import the parts one by one. Every line except `tokens.css` is required: without `tw-animate-css` and the two plugins, overlays lose their enter and exit animations and `scrollbar-hide` does nothing, and the build reports no error.

```css title="app.css"
@import 'tailwindcss';
@import 'tw-animate-css';
@import '@kortix/ui/tokens.css'; /* optional: or set every variable yourself */
@import '@kortix/ui/theme.css';
@plugin 'tailwind-scrollbar';
@plugin 'tailwind-scrollbar-hide';
```
</Tab>
<Tab title="Host without Tailwind">
Import one prebuilt file. It holds the reset, the tokens and every class the components use.

```ts title="main.tsx"
import '@kortix/ui/styles.css';
```

It holds no class that only your own code uses. Style your own markup with plain CSS that reads the variables, such as `var(--border)`.
</Tab>
</Tabs>

- Dark mode is the `dark` class on `<html>`. Add or remove it to switch themes. `useIsDark()` returns the current state.
- Both paths style the page itself: `body` paints `--background` and `--foreground`, borders default to `--border`, native controls follow the theme (`color-scheme`), text selection reads `--selection`, and interactive elements show the pointer cursor. You add no base rules of your own.
- `@kortix/ui/fonts.css` loads the Roobert font, for Kortix-owned surfaces only. Every other host renders the system font stack (`--font-sans-system`).

### Mount the providers

Mount these once, at the root of your app:

```tsx title="providers.tsx"
import { UiProvider, type UiLinkComponent } from '@kortix/ui';
import { IconProvider } from '@kortix/ui/icon-provider';
import { Toaster } from '@kortix/ui/toast';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

// Any router: `UiProvider` passes anchor props with `href` and a Next.js-style `prefetch` hint.
const RouterLink: UiLinkComponent = ({ href, prefetch: _prefetch, ...props }) => <Link to={href} {...props} />;

export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <UiProvider strings={{ confirm: 'Confirm', cancel: 'Cancel' }} link={RouterLink}>
      <IconProvider>
        {children}
        <Toaster />
      </IconProvider>
    </UiProvider>
  );
}
```

| Provider | What it does | Without it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `UiProvider` | `strings`: the copy the components render when you pass no label, for translation: `confirm`, `cancel`, `close` (the screen-reader label of the dialog and sheet close button), `commandPaletteTitle`, `commandPaletteDescription` (the session UI reads `i18n`). Pass only the keys you translate. `link`: the link component of your router, called with `href`. `useBrand`: a hook that returns your organization's logo URLs for `KortixLogo`. `image`: your image component. `navigation`: your router as four hooks (`useRouter`, `usePathname`, `useSearchParams`, `useParams`; a Next.js app passes the `next/navigation` hooks), plus `observesHistory: true` when the router observes `history.pushState` (Next.js does; React Router does not). With it, a session tab or a query param the UI changes is written to history with no navigation; without it, the change goes through `useRouter`. `i18n`: your translation hooks (`useTranslations`, `useLocale`, the next-intl signatures) or `locale` + `messages`. `useViewerId`: a hook that returns the signed-in Kortix user's id. Without it, a one-person session names no sender and a stopped turn never says "Stopped by"; a session several people write in needs it. `components`: the app features the session UI opens but does not own (`ShareSessionModal`, which `@kortix/ui/workspace` exports; `HomeAnnouncement`, `ProjectProviderModal`, `ProviderAccountsModal`, `ConnectorConnectModal`, `Wallpaper`). `config`: `managedModelsEnabled` for a deployment that serves its model lineup, `connectorsEnabled`. `config.terminalEnabled` (default true): false hides the Terminal, for a host whose proxy cannot upgrade a WebSocket. `config.cliConnectEnabled` (default true): false hides the Terminal's "Connect from your machine" bar, for a host that serves no `<origin>/install` installer and ships no Kortix CLI. `config.publicSharesEnabled` (default true): false hides every public-link control, for a host that serves no page at `share.public_path` (`/share/session/<token>`). `config.commandPaletteShortcut` and `config.settingsShortcut` (default false): true when the host opens a command palette on ⌘K or settings on ⌘,; only then does the composer hint at them. `config.providerIconBaseUrl`: where the host serves provider logos (`<base>/<icon>.svg`); without it they load from models.dev, and the Kortix provider draws `useBrand`'s icon. `config.accountApiEnabled` (default true): false for a host that fronts one operator key and blocks the account routes; then no IAM probe, billing or session-oversight request runs, billing state stays empty, and every permission gate shows its control (the server enforces each route). `resolvePreviewUrl`: an async function of the request (it returns a Promise) that resolves to the URL the session browser frames, with the host's own scoped credentials (for a host whose origin is not the Kortix backend, or whose browser must not hold a Kortix token); a `null` result or no function keeps the SDK preview URL. Billing is the SDK flag `billingEnabled` of `createKortix({ backendUrl, getToken, billingEnabled })`, which every billing gate reads. It is for Kortix's own app: its upgrade actions open the Kortix plan dialog that apps/web mounts. A custom host leaves it off, so no upgrade control renders. `UiProvider` also loads the animation features: without it, expanded rows stay closed. | English copy, a plain `<a>` and `<img>`, the Kortix logo, full page loads, no route params, the bundled English catalog of `@kortix/ui/i18n`, no viewer, billing off, no managed model lineup, no slots: a control that opens a missing slot is hidden. |
| `SessionIdsProvider` | `projectId` and `sessionId` of the view it wraps. The session UI reads them with `useSessionIds`, never from your route. `SessionThread`, `SessionWorkspace` and `ProjectHome` mount it themselves, with the SDK's project scope. Around any other block, mount it inside `KortixProjectProvider` from `@kortix/sdk/react`. | Both ids are `undefined`: components that need them render nothing. |
| `setUiTelemetry` from `@kortix/ui/lib/telemetry` | Not a provider: call it once at module load with `track`, `captureException`, `captureMessage`, `addBreadcrumb`. Declare `UiTelemetryRegister { event: YourEvents }` to make `tsc` check event names. | `track` does nothing; `captureException` logs to the console. |
| `IconProvider` | Gives every Phosphor icon the Kortix weight and size. | Phosphor's defaults. |
| `<Toaster />` from `@kortix/ui/toast` | Renders the toasts that `successToast`, `errorToast` and the other helpers in `@kortix/ui/toast` raise. | No toast appears. |

### Routes

Every URL the components link to comes from one route table, `setUiRoutes` (`@kortix/ui`), called once at module load. The defaults are Kortix's own paths: `project` `/projects/<id>`, `session` `/projects/<id>/sessions/<sessionId>`, `sessions` `/projects/<id>/sessions` (`?view=archived|deleted`), `files` `/projects/<id>/files`, `drive` `/projects/<id>/drive`, `apps` `/projects/<id>/apps`, `members` `/projects/<id>/members`, `sandboxSettings` `/projects/<id>/config?section=sandbox`, `customize` `/projects/<id>/customize/<page>` (agents, skills, connectors, triggers, reminders, models, secrets, review, settings). Return your own path for a page you serve elsewhere. Return `null` for a page you do not serve: every link to it is hidden. `project` and `session` cannot be null.

```ts
import { setUiRoutes } from '@kortix/ui';

setUiRoutes({
  session: (projectId, sessionId) => `/w/${projectId}/chat/${sessionId}`,
  drive: () => null,
  customize: () => null,
});
```

### Theming

Every value a host is expected to vary is a CSS variable: colors in light and dark, the radius scale, border width, fonts, the type scale, spacing density, shadows and motion. Limits lists the few values that stay fixed. `@kortix/ui/theme` turns a typed theme object into those variables. Every field is optional. An unset field keeps the Kortix default, and a `dark` color falls back to the Kortix dark default, never to your `light` value.

#### Bring your company brand in 10 lines

```tsx title="app/layout.tsx"
import { themeToCss, type KortixTheme } from '@kortix/ui/theme';

const brand: KortixTheme = {
  light: { blue: '#4f46e5', ring: '#4f46e5', border: '#e0e7ff' },
  dark: { blue: '#818cf8', ring: '#818cf8', border: '#312e81' },
  radius: '0.25rem',
  fonts: { sans: 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif' },
};

// <html><head><style>{themeToCss(brand)}</style></head>…
```

`themeToCss` is a pure function, so it runs on the server and the first paint is already on brand. Its blocks out-rank `tokens.css`, so the `<style>` can sit anywhere in the page. The white-label demo does exactly this: `apps/white-label/lumen/src/config/theme.ts` is its whole re-skin.

#### The API

| Export | Signature | Use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `KortixTheme` | `{ light?, dark?, radius?, spacing?, borderWidth?, fonts?, text?, lineHeights?, shadows?, durations?, easing?, vars? }` | The theme. `light` and `dark` take the color keys below plus any `--custom` variable. `vars` sets any other variable in both modes. An unknown field, color or step is a type error and throws at runtime. |
| `themeToCss` | `(theme: KortixTheme, selector = ':root') => string` | A stylesheet: light outside `.dark`, dark on or inside `.dark`. Pass a selector to theme one element. It throws on a value that contains `;`, `{`, `}`, `<`, `>`, `\` or a comment, and on a variable name other than `--[A-Za-z0-9_-]+`, so a theme built from tenant data cannot add CSS rules. |
| `themeToCssVars` | `(theme: KortixTheme) => { light: Record<string, string>; dark: Record<string, string> }` | The same variables as data, for a host that writes its own CSS or sets them from JavaScript. Mode-independent values appear in both. |
| `ThemeScope` | `<ThemeScope theme={…} className? {...divProps}>` | Themes one subtree: an embedded widget, a customer preview, a settings pane. Dialogs, popovers, menus, tooltips, selects, sheets and drawers opened inside it portal into a host at the end of `<body>` that carries the scope's theme, so they wear it too. |

```tsx title="A themed subtree"
import { ThemeScope } from '@kortix/ui/theme';

<ThemeScope theme={{ light: { primary: '#0f766e' }, radius: '1rem' }} className="bg-background">
  <SessionThread … />
</ThemeScope>
```

`ThemeScope` sets `font-family` and `color` on its element, because both inherit as computed values and would otherwise ignore the override. It paints no background; add `bg-background` when it should. Put `className="dark"` on it to force dark for the subtree. The scope element holds only your children: its `<style>` renders as the sibling just before it, and the overlay host lives in `<body>`. So `flex gap-*`, `grid`, `space-y-*` and `first:` / `last:` on its children behave as they would without the scope. Overlays are not clipped or offset by a `transform`, `filter`, `backdrop-filter`, `contain` or `overflow: hidden` ancestor of the scope, and a nested scope's overlays carry both themes.

Toasts do not portal: `<Toaster>` renders where it is mounted. To theme toasts, mount `<Toaster />` inside the `ThemeScope`, at its top level, not inside a transformed panel.

#### Tokens

Colors take any CSS color. Defaults are the Kortix values (light / dark).

| Key | Variable | Controls | Default light / dark |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `background` | `--background` | The page | `#ffffff` / `#0b0b0b` |
| `pane`, `surface` | `--pane`, `--surface` | Base and raised panes | `#ffffff` / `#0c0c0c`, `#fcfcfc` / `#141414` |
| `foreground` | `--foreground` | Text | `#1f1f1f` / `#ffffff` |
| `action`, `actionForeground` | `--action`, `--action-foreground` | The default `Button` fill and its text (primary actions, Send) | follow `foreground` / `background` |
| `foregroundStrong`, `foregroundWeak` | `--foreground-strong`, `--foreground-weak` | Emphasised and quiet text | follow `foreground` / `mutedForeground` |
| `card`, `cardForeground` | `--card`, `--card-foreground` | `Card`, chat tool cards and questions | `#f4f4f4` / `#141414`, ink |
| `popover`, `popoverForeground` | `--popover`, `--popover-foreground` | Menus, popovers, inputs | `#ffffff` / `#141414`, ink |
| `primary`, `primaryForeground` | `--primary`, `--primary-foreground` | `Progress`, `Slider`, `Calendar` selection, menu highlight, default selection | `#1f1f1f` / `#ffffff`, inverse |
| `secondary`, `secondaryForeground` | `--secondary`, `--secondary-foreground` | Secondary buttons, liquid glass | `#ededed` / `#1c1c1c`, ink |
| `muted`, `mutedForeground` | `--muted`, `--muted-foreground` | Quiet fills, the chat user bubble, code blocks, captions | `#ededed` / `#1c1c1c`, `#666666` / `#999999` |
| `accent`, `accentForeground` | `--accent`, `--accent-foreground` | Hovered rows | `#f4f4f4` / `#141414`, ink |
| `destructive`, `destructiveForeground` | `--destructive`, `--destructive-foreground` | Errors, destructive buttons | `#e7000b` / `#ff6467`, `#fafafa` |
| `border` | `--border` | Every hairline and default border color | `#e2e2e2` / `#262626` |
| `input` | `--input` | Input fills | `#ededed` / `#1c1c1c` |
| `ring` | `--ring` | Focus rings, the focused composer | `#0099ff` |
| `focusRing` | `--focus-ring` | Card focus ring | follows `ring` |
| `hover`, `active` | `--hover`, `--active` | Translucent hover and pressed tints | ink 4.5% / 7.5%, white 6% / 10% |
| `selection`, `selectionForeground` | `--selection`, `--selection-foreground` | Text selection | `primary` at 72%, `primaryForeground` |
| `sidebar…` (`sidebar`, `sidebarForeground`, `sidebarPrimary`, `sidebarPrimaryForeground`, `sidebarAccent`, `sidebarAccentForeground`, `sidebarRow`, `sidebarRowControl`, `sidebarBorder`, `sidebarRing`) | `--sidebar-*` | The sidebar; `sidebar` also fills `Dialog`, `AlertDialog`, `Modal` and `Sheet` | surface-1, ink, `ring` |
| `blue` | `--kortix-blue` | The accent: a checked `Switch` / `Checkbox` / `RadioGroup`, `brand` and `blue` `Button` variants, info `Badge`, `InfoBanner`, info status | `#2b91f7` |
| `green`, `orange`, `yellow`, `red`, `purple` | `--kortix-*` | Status tones. Keep their meaning. | `#199338`, `#d18b19`, `#cca300`, `#f14b4c`, `#ab80d6` |
| `brand` | `--kortix-base` | Kortix base accent | follows `ring` |
| `chart1` … `chart5` | `--chart-1` … `--chart-5` | Chart series | amber ramp |
| `terminalSurface`, `terminalForeground`, `terminalBorder` | `--terminal-*` | The terminal pane (always dark) | `#0f0f0f`, `#e5e5e5`, white 10% |

Shared values apply in both modes:

| Key | Variable | Controls | Default |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `borderWidth` | `--border-width` | Every `border`, `border-t` / `-x` / `-y` / … and `divide-*` line with no explicit width. The heavier 1.5× strokes (`Checkbox`, `Sheet`, the dashed chart legend) scale with it | `1px` |
| `radius` | `--radius` | Every corner. `rounded` = −6px, `-sm` −4px, `-md` −2px, `-lg` =, `-xl` +4px, `-2xl` +6px | `0.625rem` (10px) |
| `spacing` | `--spacing` | Density: every `p-*`, `m-*`, `gap-*`, `size-*` is a multiple | `0.23rem` |
| `fonts.sans` | `--font-family-sans` | Body text, headings, every `font-sans` (there is no separate heading face) | system stack (Roobert on Kortix surfaces) |
| `fonts.mono` | `--font-family-mono` | Code, ids, `Badge` | system mono stack |
| `text.xs` … `text['8xl']` | `--text-<step>` | Font size of each `text-*` step | `0.8125rem`, `0.875rem`, `0.9rem`, `1rem`, `1.125rem` … `6rem` |
| `lineHeights.<step>` | `--text-<step>--line-height` | Line height of each `text-*` step | the Kortix ratio per step |
| `shadows['2xs']` … `shadows['2xl']` | `--shadow-<step>` | Elevation of each `shadow-*` step. `0 0 #0000` flattens one; `none` would also drop a `ring-*` on that element | Tailwind's shadow ramp |
| `durations.fast` … `durations.slower` | `--duration-<step>` | `duration-fast` … `duration-slower` | `100ms`, `150ms`, `200ms`, `300ms`, `500ms` |
| `easing.default`, `in`, `out`, `inOut` | `--ease-*` | `ease-*` curves | the Kortix curves |
| `vars` | any `--name` | Anything else, for example `--liquid-glass-blur` | — |

#### Limits

- An explicit width class (`border-2`, `border-[3px]`) stays fixed. Only bare `border*` and `divide-*` follow `borderWidth`.
- Font sizes with no step stay fixed: 10px, 11px and 12px captions (the card meta line, the attachment tile, code-block chrome) and the `0.8rem` inline code chip.
- Durations with no step stay fixed: `duration-80`, `duration-0`, the sidebar slide (`200` / `240` / `260ms` on `cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)`), the command palette (`140` / `180ms`) and the enter / exit keyframes (`200` / `250ms`). Every `duration-100` / `150` / `200` / `300` / `500` reads `duration-fast` … `duration-slower`.
- A `ThemeScope` can force dark inside a light page, not light inside a dark one: no `.light` reset exists, so `.dark` on `<html>` reaches the scope.
- Chat surfaces read the semantic tokens above: `muted` for the user bubble and code blocks, `card` and `border` for tool cards and questions, `mutedForeground` for captions. They have no tokens of their own.
- A few decorative values stay fixed: the dialog scrims (black at 20–85%), the `inverse` `Button` variant, the `Tag` status palette, the loader and dot-matrix gradients, and chart legend swatches.

#### Rules

- Theme through tokens. Never fork or restyle a component: a forked copy stops receiving fixes.
- Pass your logo through `UiProvider useBrand`, so `KortixLogo` renders your marks.
- Do not load `fonts.css`: Roobert is for Kortix surfaces. Set `fonts.sans`.
- Plain CSS works too, after the `@kortix/ui` import. Put light values in `:root:not(.dark)` and dark values in `.dark`. A bare `:root` rule also matches `<html class="dark">` and, coming later than `tokens.css`, overrides the dark value. `themeToCss` writes these selectors for you.

### Components

Each component is one subpath, `@kortix/ui/<file>`. The live catalog at [kortix.com/design-system](https://kortix.com/design-system) renders every one in light and dark, with its usage rules.

| Need | Compose | Import | Live |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Actions | `Button`, `ButtonGroup`, `Toggle` | `button`, `button-group`, `toggle` | [Button](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-button), [Button Group](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-button-group) |
| Forms | `Field`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `Select`, `CheckboxGroup`, `RadioGroup`, `Switch`, `Slider`, `Label` | `field`, `input`, `textarea`, `select`, `checkbox-group`, `radio-group`, `switch`, `slider`, `label` | [Input](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-input), [Select](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-select), [Switch](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-switch) |
| Search | `InputGroupSearch`, `PageSearchBar`, `Command` | `input-group`, `page-search-bar`, `command` | [PageSearchBar](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-search-bar) |
| Overlays | `Modal`, `Sheet`, `Popover`, `DropdownMenu`, `Hint` | `modal`, `sheet`, `popover`, `dropdown-menu`, `hint` | [Modal](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-modal), [Sheet](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-sheet), [Popover](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-popover), [Dropdown](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-dropdown), [Tooltip](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-tooltip) |
| Confirm | `ConfirmDialog`, `AlertDialog` | `confirm-dialog`, `alert-dialog` | [Alert Dialog](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-alert-dialog) |
| Feedback | `InfoBanner`, the toast helpers, `Loading`, `Skeleton`, `Progress` | `info-banner`, `toast`, `loading`, `skeleton`, `progress` | [InfoBanner](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-info-banner), [Toast](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-toast), [Skeleton](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-skeleton) |
| Status | `Badge`, `CountBadge`, `StatusBadge` | `badge`, `count-badge`, `status` | [Badge](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-badge), [Status](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-status) |
| Layout | `Card`, `Section`, `Tabs`, `Disclosure`, `Accordion`, `Separator`, `ScrollArea` | `card`, `section`, `tabs`, `disclosure`, `accordion`, `separator`, `scroll-area` | [Card](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-card), [Tabs](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-tabs), [Accordion](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-accordion) |
| Data | `Table`, `DefinitionList`, `InlineMeta`, `Empty`, `Calendar` | `table`, `definition-list`, `inline-meta`, `empty`, `calendar` | [Table](https://kortix.com/design-system#comp-table), [DefinitionList](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-definition-list), [InlineMeta](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-inline-meta) |
| People | `UserAvatar`, `EntityAvatar` | `user-avatar`, `entity-avatar` | [Avatars](https://kortix.com/design-system#pat-avatars) |

Compose before you create. Use `Modal` for a dialog, `Hint` for a tooltip, `Loading` for a spinner, `Badge` for a chip and `ConfirmDialog` before a destructive action. Never call `sonner`'s `toast()` directly, and never use an icon with `animate-spin` as a spinner. Icons come from `@phosphor-icons/react`.

### Modules

Every part of a Kortix session is a module with one entry. Each entry is a curated list of names: that list is the public API, under semver. Every piece needs `UiProvider` and a `QueryClientProvider` at the root.

| Entry | What it is | Minimal mount |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `@kortix/ui/workspace` | Composed project screens: `SessionWorkspace` (thread plus resizable side panel), `ProjectHome` (the new-session composer; it sends by itself), `ProjectSessionList`, `ProjectSessionsView`, `ProjectFilesView`, `ProjectSidebar`, `ShareSessionModal` | `<SessionWorkspace session={useSession(projectId, sessionId)} />`, `<ProjectHome projectId={projectId} />`; `ProjectSessionList` inside a `SidebarProvider` |
| `@kortix/ui/session` | The conversation: `SessionThread`, turns, tool rows and `ToolRegistry`, prompts, status, `ModelSelector`, `AgentSelector` | `<SessionThread session={useSession(projectId, sessionId)} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/composer` | The message composer: `ComposerChatInput`, `SessionChatInput`, its chrome, the editor parts, the send pipeline | `<ComposerChatInput projectId={projectId} onSend={…} />` in the project scope |
| `@kortix/ui/session-panel` | The side panel: `SessionPanelProvider`, `ActionPanel`, `SessionFiles`, `SessionChanges`, `SessionAudit`, `openSessionQuickView` | `SessionPanelProvider` (runtime `sessionId`, `projectId`, `projectSessionId`) |
| `@kortix/ui/files` | Project files (the git-backed repo: `ProjectFilesProvider`, `FileExplorerPage`, `DriveExplorer`), sandbox files (`SandboxFileExplorer`), `FilePreviewHost`, drive and change-request hooks | `ProjectFilesView` from `workspace` (it fills its parent: the parent sets the height); `<FilePreviewHost />` once on a page without a side panel |
| `@kortix/ui/file-viewer` | `FileViewer` and `FileContentRenderer` for any file kind (pdf, docx, xlsx, csv, pptx, zip, images, code), `FilePreviewModal`, `FileSourceProvider` | `<FileViewer content={text} fileName="notes.md" />` |
| `@kortix/ui/terminal`, `@kortix/ui/browser` | `SessionTerminal`; `SessionBrowser`, `BrowserLanding` | the runtime ids of a mounted `useSession` |
| `@kortix/ui/markdown`, `@kortix/ui/genui` | `UnifiedMarkdown`, `MarkdownWithFrontmatter`, `HighlightedCode`; typed UI blocks in assistant text | `<UnifiedMarkdown content={text} trust="agent" />` |
| `@kortix/ui/diff`, `@kortix/ui/changes` | `DiffView`; `ChangeList` with its vocabulary | `<DiffView before={…} after={…} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/apps` | `AppPreview`, the frame pool, the App view model | none |
| `@kortix/ui/access`, `@kortix/ui/customize` | Sharing and roles (`AccessDialog`, `RoleSelect`, `PrincipalPicker`); the project configuration pages (`ConnectorsPage`, `AgentsPage`, `TriggersPage`, `SecretsPage`, `ModelsPage`, `SkillsPage`, `ProjectSettingsPage`) | `<ConnectorsPage projectId={projectId} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/theme`, `@kortix/ui/i18n`, `@kortix/ui/icons` | The typed theme; `useTranslations`, `useLocale` (next-intl signatures); the library's glyphs | none |

The package skill lists every name of every entry, its props and one example that compiles: `skills/kortix-ui/references/modules.md`.

#### Deep paths have no semver

The entries and the primitive subpaths (`@kortix/ui/<component>`) are the public API. A few deep paths also resolve: `@kortix/ui/internal/*` and `@kortix/ui/<module>/<path>`, such as `@kortix/ui/session/thread/session-chat`. They exist only for Kortix's own apps (the list is `scripts/deep-exports.json`), and they can change or disappear in any release. Import the entry. If an entry lacks a name you need, ask for it to be added to the entry.

### Add a session

`SessionWorkspace` (`@kortix/ui/workspace`) renders the whole session screen of the Kortix web app from one [`useSession`](/docs/#usesession) result: the thread, and the resizable side panel its actions open (files, the file viewer, the browser, the terminal, changes). On a phone the panel is a sheet.

```tsx title="session.tsx"
import { useSession } from '@kortix/sdk/react';
import { SessionWorkspace } from '@kortix/ui/workspace';

export function Session({ projectId, sessionId }: { projectId: string; sessionId: string }) {
  return <SessionWorkspace session={useSession(projectId, sessionId)} />;
}
```

`SessionWorkspace` takes `session`, `children` (your own column in place of the thread), `hideHeader`, `readOnly` and `className`. The parent sets the size.

#### The thread alone

`SessionThread` renders the whole chat of one session from one [`useSession`](/docs/#usesession) result. It is the thread the Kortix web app renders: the transcript, the streaming turn, tool rows, permission and question prompts, the send error, and the composer with attachments, slash commands, the model and agent pickers and Stop.

```tsx title="chat.tsx"
import { useSession } from '@kortix/sdk/react';
import { SessionThread } from '@kortix/ui/session';

export function Chat({ projectId, sessionId }: { projectId: string; sessionId: string }) {
  const session = useSession(projectId, sessionId);
  return (
    <div className="flex h-dvh flex-col">
      <SessionThread session={session} hideHeader />
    </div>
  );
}
```

The parent sets the height, and the thread fills it. Everything reads off `session`: there is no second chat hook and no transport to write. The host mounts `QueryClientProvider`, `UiProvider` and one `<Toaster />`. A control whose host feature is missing (a `UiProvider` `components` slot) is hidden, never dead. With no side panel on the page, a file or preview the thread opens shows in a dialog.

| Prop | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `session` | The `useSession(projectId, sessionId)` result. Required. |
| `hideHeader` | Hides the thread's title row. Use it when your page has its own header. |
| `readOnly` | Hides the composer: a transcript to read. |
| `className` | Classes on the thread's root. |

`@kortix/ui/session` also exports `ModelSelector` and `AgentSelector`, the composer's pickers. They are controlled (`models` or `agents`, the selection, `onSelect`), so a form outside a session, such as a new-session form, uses them too.

#### Replace one tool row

Every built-in tool row registers when `@kortix/ui/session` loads. Register your own row for a tool name to replace it. The row gets `ToolProps`: `call` is the session-log tool call (`name`, `input`, `status`, `result`). `BasicTool` draws the standard row; `GenericTool` is the default row.

```tsx title="src/shell-row.tsx"
import { BasicTool, ToolRegistry, type ToolProps } from '@kortix/ui/session';
import { TerminalWindowIcon } from '@phosphor-icons/react';

// Replaces the built-in row of the `bash` tool. A later register() for the same
// name wins; @kortix/ui/session registers its rows on import.
function ShellRow({ call }: ToolProps) {
  const command = (call.input as { command?: string } | null)?.command ?? '';
  const output = (call.result?.content ?? [])
    .map((part) => (part.type === 'text' ? part.text : ''))
    .join('');
  return (
    <BasicTool icon={<TerminalWindowIcon />} trigger={{ title: 'Shell', subtitle: command }}>
      <pre data-testid="acme-shell-output" className="bg-muted overflow-x-auto rounded-md p-3 font-mono text-xs">
        {output || 'No output yet'}
      </pre>
    </BasicTool>
  );
}

ToolRegistry.register('bash', ShellRow);
```

Import the file once at your entry (`import './shell-row'`). `call.status` is `pending`, `running`, `complete` or `error`; `call.result.content` holds the text and attachment parts.

#### Compose a custom workspace

The modules compose at any depth. Pick the level you need:

| You want | Use |
| --- | --- |
| Kortix's session screen in your chrome | `SessionWorkspace` |
| Your own conversation column beside Kortix's side panel | `<SessionWorkspace session={session}>{yourColumn}</SessionWorkspace>` |
| The thread, no panel | `SessionThread`; a page with neither mounts `<FilePreviewHost />` from `@kortix/ui/files` once |
| Your own panel | `SessionPanelProvider` + `ActionPanel` from `@kortix/ui/session-panel`, or `SessionTerminal` from `@kortix/ui/terminal` |
| Single blocks | `UserMessage`, `ThrottledMarkdown`, `ToolPartRenderer`, `ModelSelector`, `AgentSelector`, `SessionPermissionPrompt`, `QuestionPrompt` from `@kortix/ui/session`; `ComposerChatInput` from `@kortix/ui/composer` |

A block outside `SessionThread`, `SessionWorkspace` and `ProjectHome` needs the two scopes those screens mount for you:

```tsx title="ask.tsx"
import { KortixProjectProvider } from '@kortix/sdk/react';
import { SessionIdsProvider } from '@kortix/ui';
import { ComposerChatInput } from '@kortix/ui/composer';

// The composer alone, before a session exists: you decide what a send does.
// Outside SessionThread / SessionWorkspace / ProjectHome, mount the two scopes
// they mount for you: the SDK's project (model catalog) and the UI's ids.
export function Ask({ projectId, onAsk }: { projectId: string; onAsk: (text: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <KortixProjectProvider projectId={projectId}>
      <SessionIdsProvider projectId={projectId}>
        <ComposerChatInput projectId={projectId} onSend={(text) => onAsk(text)} />
      </SessionIdsProvider>
    </KortixProjectProvider>
  );
}
```

#### Render the transcript yourself

To draw every part with your own components, skip `@kortix/ui/session` and use the headless kit in `@kortix/sdk/react`: `useChatTurns` and `renderParts`. `renderParts` requires one renderer per part kind, so a new kind fails your build, not your UI. See [Build a chat UI](/docs/#build-a-chat-ui).

### Build a dashboard

A dashboard is your chrome around the composed screens: Kortix's session list in your sidebar, `ProjectHome` to start a session, `SessionWorkspace` to work in one, `ProjectFilesView` for the project's files. The steps below are files of one Vite + React 19 + react-router app that installed both packages from their packed tarballs. It passed `tsc --noEmit` and `vite build` and ran against a live Kortix API: a prompt from the home opened the session, the tool rows (the custom one included) and the reply rendered, a file opened in the side panel and on the Files page, with 0 console errors.

<Steps>
<Step title="Create the client">
Create it once, at module scope. `getToken` runs on every call, so a key never sits in the bundle.

```ts title="src/kortix.ts"
import { createKortix } from '@kortix/sdk';

const TOKEN_KEY = 'kortix_token';

export function readToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function saveToken(token: string): void {
  sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token.trim());
}

// One client per host, created once at module scope. Every @kortix/sdk/react
// hook and every @kortix/ui/session component reads it.
export const kortix = createKortix({
  backendUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_KORTIX_API_URL ?? 'https://api.kortix.com/v1',
  getToken: async () => readToken(),
});
```

The browser origin must be in the API's CORS list. In local development, proxy `/v1` through your dev server and point `backendUrl` at it. Inside a [Kortix App](/docs/#apps-reference), the client acts as the viewer: run `kortix apps access <app> --viewer api`, then `createKortix({ backendUrl: '/_kortix/api/v1', getToken: kortixAppViewerToken() })`.
</Step>
<Step title="Connect your router and theme">
```ts title="src/theme.ts"
import type { KortixTheme } from '@kortix/ui/theme';

// The company brand. Every field is optional: an unset one keeps the Kortix value.
export const acmeTheme: KortixTheme = {
  light: { blue: '#0f766e', ring: '#0f766e', border: '#cbd5e1', muted: '#f1f5f9' },
  dark: { blue: '#2dd4bf', ring: '#2dd4bf', border: '#334155', muted: '#1e293b' },
  radius: '0.375rem',
  fonts: { sans: 'Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif' },
};
```

```tsx title="src/router-adapters.tsx"
import type { UiLinkComponent, UiNavigation, UiRouter } from '@kortix/ui';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

// @kortix/ui links and router calls go through your router, not full page loads.
export const RouterLink: UiLinkComponent = ({ href, prefetch: _prefetch, ...props }) => (
  <Link to={href} {...props} />
);

function useUiRouterAdapter(): UiRouter {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return useMemo(
    () => ({
      push: (href) => navigate(href),
      replace: (href) => navigate(href, { replace: true }),
      prefetch: () => {},
      back: () => navigate(-1),
    }),
    [navigate],
  );
}

export const navigation: UiNavigation = {
  useRouter: useUiRouterAdapter,
  usePathname: () => useLocation().pathname,
  useSearchParams: () => useSearchParams()[0],
  useParams: () => useParams(),
};
```

```tsx title="src/providers.tsx"
import { UiProvider } from '@kortix/ui';
import { IconProvider } from '@kortix/ui/icon-provider';
import { themeToCss } from '@kortix/ui/theme';
import { Toaster } from '@kortix/ui/toast';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import './kortix';
import { navigation, RouterLink } from './router-adapters';
import { acmeTheme } from './theme';

const themeCss = themeToCss(acmeTheme);
// The deployment serves its managed model lineup: the model picker lists it.
const config = { managedModelsEnabled: true };

// Mount inside the router: `navigation` calls its hooks.
export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(() => new QueryClient());
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <UiProvider link={RouterLink} navigation={navigation} config={config}>
        <IconProvider>
          <style href="acme-theme" precedence="default">{themeCss}</style>
          {children}
          <Toaster />
        </IconProvider>
      </UiProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

React 19 hoists `<style href precedence>` into `<head>`. A Next.js host passes `next/link` and the `next/navigation` hooks unchanged.
</Step>
<Step title="Put the session list in your sidebar">
The list links through [the route table](#routes). This app serves the project home, its files and a session, so it maps the other pages to `null`.

```tsx title="src/project-shell.tsx"
import { SessionIdsProvider } from '@kortix/ui';
import {
  Sidebar,
  SidebarContent,
  SidebarGroup,
  SidebarHeader,
  SidebarInset,
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  SidebarProvider,
  SidebarRail,
} from '@kortix/ui/sidebar';
import { ProjectSessionList } from '@kortix/ui/workspace';
import { FolderSimpleIcon, NavigationArrowIcon, SquaresFourIcon } from '@phosphor-icons/react';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

// Every project page: your sidebar chrome around Kortix's session list.
export function ProjectShell() {
  const { projectId = '', sessionId } = useParams();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const nav = [
    { to: `/projects/${projectId}`, label: 'New session', icon: NavigationArrowIcon },
    { to: `/projects/${projectId}/files`, label: 'Files', icon: FolderSimpleIcon },
    { to: '/', label: 'All projects', icon: SquaresFourIcon },
  ];
  return (
    <SessionIdsProvider projectId={projectId} sessionId={sessionId}>
      <SidebarProvider>
        <Sidebar collapsible="offcanvas" variant="inset">
          <SidebarHeader className="px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium">Acme Agents</SidebarHeader>
          <SidebarContent className="min-h-0">
            <SidebarGroup className="py-0">
              <SidebarMenu>
                {nav.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon }) => (
                  <SidebarMenuItem key={to}>
                    <SidebarMenuButton asChild isActive={pathname === to}>
                      <Link to={to}>
                        <Icon />
                        <span>{label}</span>
                      </Link>
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                  </SidebarMenuItem>
                ))}
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroup>
            {/* Kortix's session list: sections, status, rename, delete, live poll. */}
            <SidebarGroup className="min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col py-0">
              <ProjectSessionList projectId={projectId} />
            </SidebarGroup>
          </SidebarContent>
          <SidebarRail />
        </Sidebar>
        <SidebarInset className="h-dvh min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
          <Outlet />
        </SidebarInset>
      </SidebarProvider>
    </SessionIdsProvider>
  );
}
```
</Step>
<Step title="Add the pages and routes">
```tsx title="src/pages.tsx"
import { useProjects, useSession } from '@kortix/sdk/react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@kortix/ui';
import Loading from '@kortix/ui/loading';
import { ProjectFilesView, ProjectHome, SessionWorkspace } from '@kortix/ui/workspace';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const projects = useProjects();
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex max-w-xl flex-col gap-4 p-8">
      <h1 className="text-lg font-medium">Acme Agents</h1>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Projects</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {projects.isLoading && <Loading />}
          {projects.data?.map((project) => (
            <Link key={project.project_id} to={`/projects/${project.project_id}`} className="hover:bg-accent rounded-md px-2 py-1.5">
              {project.name}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

// The project home: Kortix's composer. Sending creates the session and opens it.
export function HomePage() {
  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();
  return <ProjectHome projectId={projectId} />;
}

// The session: the thread plus the resizable side panel (files, viewer, terminal, browser).
export function SessionPage() {
  const { projectId = '', sessionId = '' } = useParams();
  const session = useSession(projectId, sessionId);
  return <SessionWorkspace key={sessionId} session={session} />;
}

// The project's git-backed files: a browser, and a viewer for any file kind.
export function FilesPage() {
  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();
  return <ProjectFilesView projectId={projectId} />;
}
```

```tsx title="src/main.tsx"
import { Button, Input, Label, setUiRoutes } from '@kortix/ui';
import { StrictMode, useState } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { createBrowserRouter, Outlet, RouterProvider } from 'react-router-dom';

import './app.css';
import './shell-row';
import { readToken, saveToken } from './kortix';
import { FilesPage, HomePage, ProjectsPage, SessionPage } from './pages';
import { ProjectShell } from './project-shell';
import { Providers } from './providers';

function SignIn() {
  const [token, setToken] = useState(readToken());
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  if (token) return <Outlet />;
  return (
    <form
      className="mx-auto flex max-w-sm flex-col gap-3 p-8"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        saveToken(draft);
        setToken(draft.trim());
      }}
    >
      <Label htmlFor="token">Kortix API key</Label>
      <Input id="token" type="password" value={draft} onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)} />
      <Button type="submit" disabled={!draft.trim()}>
        Open dashboard
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

// This router serves the default paths of `project`, `files` and `session`. Every link to
// another page is hidden.
setUiRoutes({
  sessions: () => null,
  drive: () => null,
  apps: () => null,
  members: () => null,
  sandboxSettings: () => null,
  customize: () => null,
});

const router = createBrowserRouter([
  {
    element: (
      <Providers>
        <SignIn />
      </Providers>
    ),
    children: [
      { path: '/', element: <ProjectsPage /> },
      {
        path: '/projects/:projectId',
        element: <ProjectShell />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
          { path: 'files', element: <FilesPage /> },
          { path: 'sessions/:sessionId', element: <SessionPage /> },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]);

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <RouterProvider router={router} />
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

`ProjectHome` creates the session with the prompt and navigates to it; `beforeSend` gates the send, `onSend` replaces it. `./shell-row` is the custom tool row of [Replace one tool row](#replace-one-tool-row).
</Step>
</Steps>

- `useProjectSessions`, `useProjectApps`, `useProjectTriggers`, `useChangeRequests`, `useGatewayOverview` and `useCan` add more data ([Hooks](/docs/#hooks)).
- Never `fetch` the Kortix API yourself. If the SDK lacks a call you need, that is an SDK gap to report.
- Three white-label examples are built this way ([overview](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label)):
  - [Lumen Workspace](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/reskin) (reskin): the Kortix workspace UI under its own theme, with the browser on a Kortix API key (direct mode) or behind its own server (wrapper mode).
  - [Team Console](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/team-console) (internal tool): a static Kortix App over one account, signed in as the viewer.
  - [Lumen](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/lumen) (your own SaaS): a legal AI product with its own users, data and billing, and Kortix as the agent backend.

### For agents

The package ships a skill at `packages/ui/skills/kortix-ui/SKILL.md`, and every Kortix session loads it as the `kortix-ui` skill. It tells an agent how to set up the package, which component to compose, which primitives are banned, and how to verify a page. Its references are `custom-dashboard.md` (this page's dashboard as one checklist: install, client, router adapters, theme, sidebar, pages, a custom tool row, an App's sign-in, verify), `modules.md` (every entry with its props and an example) and `theming.md` (every theme key). In a Kortix session, an agent reads it with `kortix system-skills file kortix-ui references/custom-dashboard.md`. Point your own coding agent at both files before it writes UI with `@kortix/ui`.

### Next steps
